- **de-arbeitszeugnis-decoder**: Ein deutsches Arbeitszeugnis entschlüsseln: die codierten Formulierungen in die Note übersetzen, die sie signalisieren, Aufbau und Schlussformel prüfen, Auslassungen und versteckte Kritik erkennen und eine Bitte um Berichtigung entwerfen. Verwenden bei Fragen wie: was bedeutet mein Arbeitszeugnis, welche Note hat mein Zeugnis, ist 'stets zu unserer vollen Zufriedenheit' gut, oder wie bitte ich um eine Korrektur meines Zeugnisses. Liefert eine Lesart Satz für Satz mit den jeweiligen Noten, eine Einschätzung der Gesamtnote, eine Liste fehlender oder schädlicher Elemente, Formulierungsvorschläge… ([mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills](https://github.com/mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills-i18n/de/de-arbeitszeugnis-decoder) · ★1.6k)

- **de-at-recht**: This skill should be used for questions on German (DE) and Austrian (AT) law - statutes, deadlines, remedies (Widerspruch, Einspruch, Beschwerde, Revision), court systems, EU social security coordination (Regulation 883/2004, family benefits, S1), cross-border DE/AT cases (Kindergeld vs. Familienbeihilfe, Differenzzahlung, residence and tax tie-breakers), and for drafting letters to German or Austrian authorities. Trigger terms - Bescheid, Frist, Widerspruch, Familienkasse, Finanzamt, ZBFS, ÖGK, SVS, RIS, Kindergeld, Familienbeihilfe, Meldezettel, Hauptwohnsitz, DBA, SOLVIT. - **de-bruijn-contig-kmer-indexing**: Use when compressing long erroneous PacBio/ONT reads against accurate DBG contigs with DBG2OLC, or when a contig-to-k-mer lookup must exclude ambiguous multi-contig k-mers before read anchoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-contig-kmer-indexing))
- **de-bruijn-graph-assembly-parameterization**: Use when configuring de novo transcriptome assembly of Illumina RNA-Seq reads and you need to compute (rather than hand-pick) k-mer sizes for iterative de Bruijn graph construction from the read length. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-assembly-parameterization))
- **de-bruijn-graph-baseline-variant-calling**: Use when benchmarking a reference-free variant caller against the de Bruijn graph on simulated or real WGS reads, needing an independent baseline call set for hap.py validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-baseline-variant-calling))
- **de-bruijn-graph-construction**: Use when assembling de novo transcriptome data and the goal is an isoform-preserving assembly graph from Illumina FASTQ reads, rather than a genome-oriented contig set that collapses alternative splicing paths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-construction))
- **de-bruijn-graph-construction**: Use when assembling Illumina short reads de novo and peak memory during de Bruijn graph construction is limiting, or when benchmarking sparse-vs-dense DBG memory tradeoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-construction))
- **de-bruijn-graph-construction-from-window-sequences**: Use when polishing MSA-derived consensus sequences (or contigs) that still contain errors, and a local de Bruijn graph over a single window's sequences can recover low-quality regions via solid k-mer anchoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-construction-from-window-sequences))
- **de-bruijn-graph-contig-scaffolding**: Use when assembling Illumina GA short reads (FASTA/FASTQ/BAM, paired-end or mate-pair libraries) into a de novo draft genome for large plant/animal or human-sized genomes and you need scaffold N50 and error metrics comparable to SOAPdenovo1, ALLPATHS-LG, or Assemblathon1/GAGE benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-contig-scaffolding))
- **de-bruijn-graph-genotype-calling**: Use when raw FASTQ reads (Illumina MiSeq or high-error-rate ONT MinION) from S. aureus or M. tuberculosis isolates must be converted directly into an antibiotic-resistance call set without assembly or SNP-calling pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-genotype-calling))
- **de-bruijn-graph-k-mer-parameter-selection**: Use when running rnaSPAdes (or SPAdes-family assemblers) on Illumina RNA-Seq reads and you must decide the multi-k schedule: lower k ≈ read_length/3 (min 29) and upper k ≈ largest odd number ≤ read_length/2 − 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-k-mer-parameter-selection))
- **de-bruijn-graph-kmer-parameterization**: Use when assembling metagenomic (environmental) FASTQ reads with MetaCortex and a maximum k-mer size must be chosen and baked in at compile time before building the de Bruijn graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-kmer-parameterization))
- **de-bruijn-graph-metagenomic-assembly**: Use when assembling metagenomic (environmental) FASTQ reads from samples with high strain-level diversity — e.g. viral mock communities, HIV lab mixes, or gut microbiomes — and both a consensus contig set and per-strain graph paths are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-metagenomic-assembly))
- **de-bruijn-graph-peptide-assembly**: Use when a set of de novo MS/MS peptide predictions (multi-enzymatic antibody digests) must be stitched into full-length light/heavy chain contigs without a reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-peptide-assembly))
- **de-bruijn-graph-postorder-traversal**: Use when you must decompose a colored (multi-sample) de Bruijn graph into sample-specific superbubbles, assign offset values per node, and build the cSupB tree used for graph coordinates and variant detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-postorder-traversal))
- **de-bruijn-graph-probe-matching**: Use when raw FASTQ reads from mycobacterial or staphylococcal isolates need rapid phylo_group, species, and lineage calls — e.g. distinguishing MTBC from NTM or concordance-checking lineage assignments against a reference caller. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-probe-matching))
- **de-bruijn-graph-short-read-preassembly**: Use when starting a DBG2OLC hybrid assembly and you have ~50x Illumina FASTQ/FASTQ.gz short reads that must first be converted into raw DBG contigs (Contigs.txt) before overlap/layout with long reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/de-bruijn-graph-short-read-preassembly))

- **deaccumulate**: Convert a forecast variable stored as a running total since the forecast start (init) into per-step values along its lead (step) axis; precipitation amounts become mm day-1 rates. ([rhiza-research/weather-skills](https://github.com/rhiza-research/weather-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/deaccumulate) · ★6)
- **deactivate**: Turn this Claude Code session off for the AgentMesh; it keeps the tools, receives nothing, and releases the work it owns. ([p2ratik/agentmesh](https://github.com/p2ratik/agentmesh/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentmesh/skills/deactivate) · ★8)
- **deactivate**: Reverse `/vsdd-factory:activate` — remove the orchestrator default agent, the platform activation state, and the generated `hooks.json`. Leaves the plugin enabled; only the default persona and per-machine hooks config are cleared. ([BOHICA-LABS/vsdd-factory](https://github.com/BOHICA-LABS/vsdd-factory/tree/HEAD/plugins/vsdd-factory/skills/deactivate) · ★2)
- **deactivate**: Deactivate SisyClaude by removing the `sisyclaude` shell alias and deleting ~/.claude/SISYCLAUDE_SYSTEM_PROMPT.md. ([Whiskey-Tango-Foxtrot-GmbH/sisyclaude](https://github.com/Whiskey-Tango-Foxtrot-GmbH/sisyclaude/tree/HEAD/skills/deactivate) · ★2)

- **deai-adapt-xiaohongshu**: 将母稿改写为小红书适配版本。使用时：需要将审核通过的终稿改写为适合小红书平台风格的笔记。小红书是种草社区：封面决定点击、标题决定曝光、正文决定收藏、评论决定流量。改写核心：搜索词布局（笔记是小红书搜索流量入口）、清单化/步骤化结构、实用价值导向（收藏驱动）、AI 辅助创作声明（平台 2025-02 起强制标识，未标识限流）。视觉要素：封面图提示词（3:4）+ 内容简介 + 文中配图。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-adapt-xiaohongshu) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-adapt-zhihu**: 将母稿改写为知乎适配版本。使用时：需要将审核通过的终稿改写为适合知乎平台风格的文章。增加分析深度、补充数据引用、使用提问式标题、以开放式讨论结尾。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-adapt-zhihu) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-analytics**: 自媒体发布数据回刷与选题反馈闭环。使用时：文章已发布到各平台 3-7 天后，需要回刷阅读/点赞/评论/收藏数据、评估哪些内容有效，并反馈给选题环节优化评分权重。覆盖发布记录补全、效果维度分析（标题类型/体裁/选题来源/平台）、爆款复盘（对比爆款指数预测 vs 实际数据）。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-analytics) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-author-voice**: 作者观点蒸馏（挖三观）。使用时：需要在流水线末尾向作者提问，深挖作者对事物/问题的真实看法与观点，把作者的"三观"蒸馏成可复用的写作主脑（观点底座/价值观图谱/表达武器库）。基于苏格拉底式提问、Laddering 阶梯法、施瓦茨价值观理论等深度访谈方法论设计，目标是让每一篇文章都能表达"作者自己的观点"而不是网上人云亦云。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-author-voice) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-blog-editor**: Edit or review any user-facing text (blog posts, articles, UI copy, marketing copy, emails) to strip out common "AI writing" tells (based on Wikipedia's "Signs of AI writing" field guide) so it reads as natural human writing. Use this whenever the user asks to "de-AI" text, make writing sound more human, review a draft for AI-sounding language, edit a blog before publishing, or whenever writing/reviewing any text for the app. ([ravikings/nativegate](https://github.com/ravikings/nativegate/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/deai-blog-editor))
- **deai-compliance**: 自媒体内容的平台合规审查与自动化特征规避。使用时：文章已通过 deai-review 质量门禁，准备进入平台适配或发布前，需要确认①AI 辅助创作声明是否合规②是否存在被平台判定为"非真人自动化创作"的机器特征③发布节奏是否有人工介入痕迹。覆盖知乎清朗行动/小红书 AI 标识/微信"非真人自动化创作行为"条款等平台规则。产出合规自检表，与 deai-daily-pipeline 的 Step 10.5 联动。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-compliance) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-consult**: 咨询方案设计与交付。使用时：需要为客户/企业产出结构化咨询方案（诊断报告/解决方案/实施计划）时。基于培训咨询知识库咨询方法论（Block 完美咨询流程、Weinberg 咨询的奥秘、德鲁克咨询思维），流程：接洽→诊断→方案→实施→复盘。产出咨询方案书、诊断报告、实施路线图。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-consult) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-course**: 企业培训课程开发。使用时：需要将专业经验/文章内容转化为结构化培训课程（企业内训/公开课/在线课）时。基于培训咨询知识库方法论（UBD 逆向设计、五星教学法、ADDIE、TTT 课程开发链条），流程：需求分析→学习目标→课程结构（五星教学法）→课件大纲→活动设计→评估设计。产出课程大纲、讲师手册要点、课件分页。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-course) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-daily-pipeline**: 每日自媒体内容生产流水线总编排。使用时：需要执行完整的"热点采集→选题→3选题×9步流水线→5平台适配"日常内容生产时。本技能是 deai-hotspot/topic/research/outline/title/write/polish/review/adapt-* 系列技能的总调度器，固化执行顺序、质量门禁与历史教训。自动化任务与手动触发均可调用本技能。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-daily-pipeline) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deai-hotspot**: 从多个平台采集热点话题（微博、百度、知乎问题热榜、知乎推荐问题、知乎邀请问题、知乎想法话题、头条、B站、抖音、百度指数、知微事见、IT之家、澎湃新闻、网易新闻、InfoQ中国、虎嗅、品玩）。使用时：需要获取今日热点榜单作为选题依据。跨来源去重、热度归一化，输出融合排名。 ([lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill](https://github.com/lornshrimp/Lorn.TechProductManagerContentCreatorSkill/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/deai-hotspot) · ★2 · 2 copies)
